country_event = {
	id = 6001
	title = "EVTNAME6001"
	desc = "EVTDESC6001"
	picture = "Rebellion"
	trigger = {
		ABU = {
			exists = no
		}
		any_owned_province = {
			is_core = ABU
		}
		any_owned_province = {
			NOT = {
				is_core = ABU
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			any_owned_province = {
				is_core = ABU
				controlled_by_rebels = no
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 6
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA6001"
		release = ABU
		ABU = {
			any_pop = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_accepted_culture = yes
						is_primary_culture = yes
					}
				}
				militancy = -10
			}
		}
		casus_belli = {
			target = ABU
			type = conquest_for_all
			months = 12
		}
	}
}

	desc = "EVTDESC6389"
	picture = "Rebellion"
	trigger = {
		YZW = {
			exists = no
		}
		any_owned_province = {
			is_core = YZW
		}
		any_owned_province = {
			NOT = {
				is_core = YZW
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			any_owned_province = {
				is_core = YZW
				controlled_by_rebels = no
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 6
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA6389"
		release = YZW
		YZW = {
			any_pop = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_accepted_culture = yes
						is_primary_culture = yes
					}
				}
				militancy = -10
			}
		}
		casus_belli = {
			target = YZW
			type = conquest_for_all
			months = 12
		}
	}
}
